# Runtime Data Ledger rev 3 - group ecs-schedule

Part of the [runtime data ledger, rev 3](../RUNTIME-DATA-LEDGER.md) (the index: vocabularies, totals, entity model, kernel features, decisions). **Tree:** `D:/wt/joltab`, `merge/ke16-into-ecsnative` @ `d11962a9`. **Rows:** 166, of which **166 active**; 666 non_rows (kept in the group JSON ledger).

Active forms: kernel-internal 108, relation 18, diagnostics 14, system-scratch 8, scope-arena 6, component 6, event 4, dense-component 2. Active classes: K 35, R 9, F 6, S 6, B 96, D 14. Crates: boyko_ecs 166. Rungs: R2 142, R5 6, R6 18.

## Contents

- [Findings](#findings)
- [Kernel features](#kernel-features)
- [Primitives refuted](#primitives-refuted)
- [Plan conflicts](#plan-conflicts)
- [Rows](#rows)

## Findings

- On this tree, scoped tasks do not allocate a cell per task. What remains per `Schedule::run` is one `Box<ScopeShared>` plus the ScopeBlock chunk allocations, which are freed after every scope. The number of allocations per run was not counted, because that needs a build.
- `FilteredAccessSet.bit_owners` allocates 24 KB on every `System::initialize`. `run_system_once` and `run_cached_system` (system_api.rs:41, :146) also reach it at runtime. A u8 param index removes it.
- Stale comments. schedule.rs:438 says "no per-frame Arc::clone", but :441 clones on every run. executor_scratch.rs:278 says "consumes by value", but the code drains. component_set.rs:85 and query_type_registry.rs:25-39 still describe the old RwLock/Mutex&lt;HashMap> registries.

## Plan conflicts

- *rows:* all builder rows (ScheduleBuilder fields, try_build / tarjan / kahn / flatten locals) and the compiled K tables *plan_a:* D:/wt/joltab/docs/ARCH-AUDIT-ECS-DATA-REMEDIATION.md:32 "- **Stage 6:** document accepted exceptions (input HashMap, ECS-core cold registries/scheduler build-maps)." *plan_b:* Owner order 2026-09-10 (brief): move ALL runtime data structures onto our system; class K is NOT exempt any more. *flag:* The remediation audit accepts scheduler build-maps and cold registries as exceptions; the owner's later order withdraws that. Recorded, not resolved here. The hot-path exception (D:/wt/joltab/docs/HOT-PATH-EXCEPTIONS.md:120 "Lowers the authored schedule into the flat id-indexed tables the executor runs on.") is about the hot-path type ban, not the allocator, so it does not conflict with moving the builder.
- *rows:* Schedule.systems / system_conditions / system_gating_sets / set_conditions / state_entries, ConflictGraph, ExecutorScratch, CompletionChannel *plan_a:* D:/claude/BoykoEngine/docs/memory/ALLOCATOR-DESIGN-SPACE.md:349 "one `TableSet` per `Schedule` ("ScheduleTables")" (TableSet per Schedule, ErasedSystem over the Heap) *plan_b:* this ledger: per-system authored data on system entities (dense-component / component / relation, KF-system-entities); compiled tables kernel-internal on VmColumn / LiveBitmap *flag:* Same kernel-internal FORM for the compiled tables (TableSet is a shape of the Column class); the conflict is (a) where per-system data lives (entity vs table) and (b) Heap / HeapDyn / TableSet as new primitives beside ComponentPool.
- *rows:* SystemBox::system and every Box&lt;dyn System> / BoolSystem site *plan_a:* D:/claude/BoykoEngine/docs/memory/ALLOCATOR-DESIGN-SPACE.md:350 "`ErasedSystem { data: NonNull<u8>, vtable: &'static SystemVTable }` (the `Task` pattern)" (bytes in the Heap) *plan_b:* this ledger: KF-erased-record-column shared with CommandQueue *flag:* Both are thin erased handles; the conflict is the backing (a general size-class Heap vs one record column that CommandQueue also uses).
- *rows:* ScheduleBuilder fields *plan_a:* D:/claude/BoykoEngine/docs/memory/ALLOCATOR-DESIGN-SPACE.md:351 "`HeapVec`, `SortedMap<TypeId/SetId, u32>` (n ≤ hundreds, build-time)" *plan_b:* this ledger: relations / components on system and set entities; TypeIntern for the label map; ScratchColumns for build transients *flag:* HeapVec / SortedMap / HeapDyn are new primitives; every ECS form above them fits these rows.
- *rows:* TermList (term_list.rs:141-198) *plan_a:* D:/claude/BoykoEngine/docs/memory/ALLOCATOR-DESIGN-SPACE.md:329 "`HeapDyn`/`HeapVec` (epoch-rate) / `FrameVec` (per call)" (HeapDyn / HeapVec) *plan_b:* this ledger: kernel-internal rotated VmColumns with a single-builder claim *flag:* The allocator design keeps a free-and-reallocate shape on its Heap; this ledger removes the per-rebuild allocation by protocol.
- *rows:* traverse_iter.rs:51 / :284 / :300 / :314 *plan_a:* D:/claude/BoykoEngine/docs/memory/ALLOCATOR-DESIGN-SPACE.md:329 "`HeapDyn`/`HeapVec` (epoch-rate) / `FrameVec` (per call)" (FrameVec per call) *plan_b:* this ledger: caller-owned system-scratch *flag:* FrameArena is dispatcher-thread-only by the allocator design's own Frame row; DescendantsIter takes `&EcsMaster`. That all holders are on the dispatcher thread is not verified; if any is not, FrameVec is unsound there.
- *rows:* Schedule::run install, try_dispatch_ready spawn, par_iter / par_chunk scopes *plan_a:* D:/claude/BoykoEngine/docs/memory/ALLOCATOR-DESIGN-SPACE.md:113 "replaces `ScopeBlock`'s `std::alloc` chunks and `Box<ScopeShared>`" (ScopeArena keyed by worker id) *plan_b:* D:/claude/BoykoEngine/docs/memory/ALLOCATOR-DESIGN-SPACE.md:461 "`WORKER_ID_DISPATCHER` is a ROLE shared by every installing thread, not a thread identity." *flag:* Not two plans disagreeing: the design and its own critique. The critique's CONFIRMED finding blocks the design as written; these rows adopt the scope-arena form with the thread-identity fix.
- *rows:* system_meta.rs:140 / :331 (gpu_intent) *plan_a:* recount3 destination Resource+VmColumn (ecs-schedule.verified.json) *plan_b:* this ledger: component on the system entity (KF-system-entities) *flag:* Not a plan document; recorded so the recount's destination is not silently overridden. It stays as the fallback if KF-system-entities is refused.

| crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/iters/query/state.rs:89 `culled_ids: Vec<ArchetypeId>,` | EnableCull::culled_ids | `Vec<ArchetypeId>` | K | kernel-internal | system | VmReservation-raw | D:/wt/joltab/docs/DENSE-ENABLE-QUERY-PLAN.md:116 "`dense_update` reseeds `matched_ids` from live `arch_presence` on every call (so dense inserts," \| D:/wt/joltab/docs/DENSE-ENABLE-QUERY-PLAN.md:136 "`enable_driver_ids` (state.rs ~L602): the `HAS_ENABLE_TERM && !IS_CANDIDATE_SEEDED` arm already" | [field; growth highwater; addr_cached no; rung R2] ECS: EnableCull::culled_ids: empty for non-enable shapes, recull = clear + extend. component/dense-component skipped: archetypes are not entities (ArchetypeId indexes ArchetypeMaster) and a query is not an entity in this kernel. enable-state skipped: the datum is an id list, not a per-entity boolean. relation skipped: a query->archetype edge would need archetypes as entities. event skipped: a memoised cache, not a message. resource-column skipped: one per query shape (per system param / per-world cold cache), not a singleton table. system-scratch skipped: memoised across runs and rebuilt only on a generation/epoch change, not rebuilt every run. scope-arena skipped: outlives any pool scope. => kernel-internal (the query's archetype-match cache is the kernel's own registry) on VmColumn&lt;ArchetypeId> (Copy); slices are borrowed under &self only, so address stability is not load-bearing. owning_entity = the system whose query param state owns the QueryState (also EcsMaster's per-world cold query cache). [rev2 GAP 6] ecs_form diagnostics (new explicit form, decided by performance): cold diagnostic text and error payloads, built only on error / report paths, so the cost that matters is I-cache and the emitting thread's time, not allocation speed. Not kernel-internal: it is not the kernel's storage bookkeeping (most of these rows are in boyko_app and boyko_render), and moving it out lets the gate on kernel-internal close. growth once; addr_cached no; rung R2] ECS: SystemBox::system: THE per-system object. component skipped: one ComponentId per concrete system type cannot hold them (crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/component/component_registry/mod.rs:63 "pub const MAX_COMPONENTS: usize = 512;" vs crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/schedule/schedule_builder.rs:71 "pub const MAX_SYSTEMS_PER_SCHEDULE: usize = 1024;", and every closure is a distinct type), and one archetype per system would scatter them. dense-component skipped: a dense column holds exactly one element type, the objects are heterogeneous `dyn System` layouts. enable-state skipped: the datum is not a boolean. relation skipped: the datum is an owned object with a vtable, not an edge between entities. event skipped: it lives for the whole Schedule and is read every frame, it is not a message consumed once. resource-column skipped: a typed column cannot hold heterogeneous layouts, and the Schedule is not a Resource (App owns it: crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/app/app.rs:140 "schedule: Option&lt;Schedule>,"). system-scratch skipped: lifetime is the Schedule, not one system run. scope-arena skipped: lifetime is the Schedule, not one pool scope. => kernel-internal: the bytes of the erased system/condition object are records in KF-erased-record-column (address-stable, drop-aware, heterogeneous Layout, thin handle = offset + &'static vtable), the same record format CommandQueue already uses for erased closures (crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/commands/command_queue.rs:83 "pub(crate) bytes: Vec&lt;MaybeUninit&lt;u8>>,"); the handle is stored in the SystemBox dense component of the system (or condition) entity. Nothing per-frame: allocated once at add_system / run_if, read every frame through the handle. DynArena is kept only as this merged kernel feature, not as a second arena beside CommandQueue. Class: One heap object per system, allocated at add_system (schedule_builder.rs:183), lives for the Schedule. The executor caches `*mut SystemBox` (the slot, schedule.rs:1307), not the boxed pointee, so the pointee need only be stable for the Schedule's life.
